**Awesome Vibe Coding Websites** is a free, open library of 344 original vibe coding website templates and 720 live demos, built by 13 AI models — GLM-5.4, GPT-6.2 Sol, GPT-6.2 Astra, Gemini 4 Carbon, Gemini 4 Neon, Kimi K3.1 Pro, GPT-6.1 Sol, Claude Opus 5.5, Grok 4.7, GPT-6 Astra, Claude Sonnet 5.5, DeepSeek V4.1 Flash and GLM-5.3. Every template ships the exact prompt that built its demo and a DESIGN.md, so you can paste it into Claude Code, Cursor, Codex or any other AI coding tool and get the same kind of site: landing pages, SaaS sites, portfolios, online stores, dashboards, 3D scenes and browser games. Preview every site live on [VibeFather](https://vibefather.ai/?utm_source=github&utm_medium=readme&utm_campaign=awesome-vibe-coding-websites&utm_content=intro).

It's made for developers, designers, indie hackers and anyone who builds websites with AI. Browse by site type, style or AI model, see how Claude, GPT, Gemini and the other models handle the same front-end prompt side by side, and start your next project from a prompt that has already been tested. The sites use real front-end stacks — React, Tailwind CSS, Motion, Three.js, Plain HTML and GSAP — and each one was checked on desktop and mobile before it went live.

**Categories:** [Landing Pages & Product Launches](#landing-pages--product-launches) <sub>13</sub> · [SaaS, AI & App Sites](#saas-ai--app-sites) <sub>75</sub> · [Games, 3D & Interactive Toys](#games-3d--interactive-toys) <sub>45</sub> · [Interactive Explainers & Slides](#interactive-explainers--slides) <sub>36</sub> · [Portfolios, Studios & Blogs](#portfolios-studios--blogs) <sub>41</sub> · [E-commerce & Product Pages](#e-commerce--product-pages) <sub>39</sub> · [Local Business & Services](#local-business--services) <sub>66</sub> · [Restaurants, Hotels & Travel](#restaurants-hotels--travel) <sub>12</sub> · [Events & Communities](#events--communities) <sub>10</sub> · [More Landing Pages](#more-landing-pages) <sub>7</sub>

1. **Copy a prompt.** Open any template folder, copy `prompt.md`, replace the `[VARIABLES]` with your own brand, copy and colors, and paste it into Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, Windsurf, Gemini CLI or another coding agent.
2. **Or pull it from your terminal:**
   ```bash
   npx vibefather add <slug>        # writes prompt.md and DESIGN.md into ./vibefather/<slug>/
   ```
3. **Or give your agent the Skill**, so it can search and build templates by itself:
   ```bash
   npx skills add liyupi/awesome-vibe-coding-websites
   ```
4. **Or connect the MCP server** `https://mcp.vibefather.ai` (tools: `search_templates`, `get_prompt`, `get_design_md`).

Every `prompt.md` has the same shape — **Brief → Stack → Variables → Design → Pages & sections → Assets → Don'ts** — so your agent knows exactly what to change and what to keep.

## FAQ

### What is vibe coding?
Vibe coding means describing the software you want in plain language and letting an AI coding agent write the code. The prompts here are written for exactly that: each one fixes the stack, pages, sections, copy, colors, fonts and motion, so the agent builds a complete, polished website instead of a generic first draft.

### Which AI models built these websites?
GLM-5.4 (45 sites), GPT-6.2 Sol (30 sites), GPT-6.2 Astra (5 sites), Gemini 4 Carbon (15 sites), Gemini 4 Neon (59 sites), Kimi K3.1 Pro (105 sites), GPT-6.1 Sol (20 sites), Claude Opus 5.5 (67 sites), Grok 4.7 (20 sites), GPT-6 Astra (10 sites), Claude Sonnet 5.5 (36 sites), DeepSeek V4.1 Flash (179 sites) and GLM-5.3 (129 sites). Every live demo is labeled with the model and the coding tool that built it, and 24 comparison pages show two models side by side on the same prompt.

### Are the prompts free?
243 of the 344 prompts are free to read and use for personal, non-commercial projects (CC BY-NC 4.0), and every DESIGN.md is free. 101 Pro templates publish their brief, stack and variables here; the full prompt, the source code of each live demo and a commercial license come with [VibeFather Pro](https://vibefather.ai/pricing?utm_source=github&utm_medium=readme&utm_campaign=awesome-vibe-coding-websites&utm_content=faq).

### Will my site look like the demo?
Each demo was built from exactly the prompt in its folder, with nothing added by hand. Run the same prompt with a capable model and you get a very close result; change the variables and the agent rewrites copy, imagery and details around your own content.

## How these sites are made

- Every site is **original**: written and generated for VibeFather with AI coding agents, then checked on desktop and mobile before it goes live.
- Inspiration is credited inside each template. We never copy another site's code, copy, images or logos, and no template is named after a brand.
- Each demo was built from the exact prompt you see here — nothing added.
- This repository is regenerated from [vibefather.ai](https://vibefather.ai/?utm_source=github&utm_medium=readme&utm_campaign=awesome-vibe-coding-websites&utm_content=footer) every day, so new templates and new models show up here automatically. Watch → Custom → Releases to hear about new models.
